- **fleet-monitor**: Fleet monitor for dmarz's experiments on 2026-10-04 (halcyon). The watcher it used is `scripts/fleet-watch.py` in the private agentops repo. Run launches moved to dmarz/orbital-orchestrator. ([dmarzzz/swarm-dynamics-lab](https://github.com/dmarzzz/swarm-dynamics-lab/blob/HEAD/lab/researchers/dmarz/agents/fleet-monitor.md) · ★3)
- **fleet-monitor**: Read-only monitor that inspects the fleet task board (.context/ files or the agent-handoff blackboard) and reports stuck, failed or retry-storming tasks. Use when the user asks for fleet status, stuck agents or task-board anomalies. Example: 'which fleet tasks are stuck?'. ([savethepolarbears/agenthaus-marketplace](https://github.com/savethepolarbears/agenthaus-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/fleet-commander/agents/fleet-monitor.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-naive-user**: Plays a first-time user who has never seen the app: tries the plain-language tasks it is given by looking at the screen in the parent session's pane, clicking and typing like a person, and returns where it got lost, what it misread and what it looked for and did not find, as bounded JSON. It has no access to the code or the page source. Use for a blind usability check; one spawn per user, one at a time. ([makarasty/makarasty-claude](https://github.com/makarasty/makarasty-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-naive-user.md))
- **fleet-node-deployer**: Deploy and manage PMOVES services on the LOCAL fleet (SPARK, 5090, 4090, Z890, Knuckles/B850, RDNA4, KVMs) via per-node Docker MCP Toolkit gateway + Tailscale-SSH remote exec. The fleet-node analog of vps-deployer. ([POWERFULMOVES/PMOVES.AI](https://github.com/POWERFULMOVES/PMOVES.AI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-node-deployer.md) · ★7)
- **fleet-open-actor**: Perform the fleet round's open leg — run `open-queue.py --json` once, write its rendered block to this round's payload, run every selected row through `/supervisor:open` using its `selected[].name` so each admitted row opens, and return the ≤ 1 KB verdict hand-back. Dispatched by router row 16 of /supervisor:fleet-drive (armed by /supervisor:fleet-loop, or run by hand). It acts; a read-only execution is not a run of it. ([bborbe/claude-supervisor](https://github.com/bborbe/claude-supervisor/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-open-actor.md))
- **fleet-orchestrator**: Runs work across the fleet by dispatching sessions and verifying what they return. ([CharlesYWL/copilot-fleet](https://github.com/CharlesYWL/copilot-fleet/blob/HEAD/apps/node/agents/fleet-orchestrator.agent.md) · ★3)
- **fleet-orchestrator**: Coordinates parallel repair work across TiltCheck subagents using the fleet manifest. ([TiltCheck-ME/tiltcheck-monorepo](https://github.com/TiltCheck-ME/tiltcheck-monorepo/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/fleet-orchestrator.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-orchestrator**: Scanner agent that reads task-status and build-plan, and computes the next eligible, file-disjoint cohort of build-plan tasks for dispatch via the fleet-dispatch Workflow ([richardhcli/akasha](https://github.com/richardhcli/akasha/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-orchestrator.md))
- **fleet-pattern-analyzer**: Analyze cross-tenant patterns and detect systemic issues from aggregated coverage data. Receives per-org results from org-coverage-reporter agents and identifies platform degradation, coordinated enrollments, SLA compliance patterns, risk concentration, silent sensor patterns, and temporal correlations. Returns fleet-wide summary with actionable recommendations. - **fleet-planner**: Implementation planner. Reads the issue, explores the codebase and guidebooks, and produces a concrete step-by-step implementation plan with architectural decisions. Stays alive to answer questions from dev and reviewer. ([hubertciebiada/fleet-commander](https://github.com/hubertciebiada/fleet-commander/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-planner.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **fleet-planner**: Use to design an agent fleet for a goal — proposes the org chart, each member's role, specialty and area, and a model and effort per member — before the plan is confirmed with the user. Read-only. ([khuonghung/claude-agent-fleet](https://github.com/khuonghung/claude-agent-fleet/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-fleet/agents/fleet-planner.md) · ★3)
- **fleet-planner**: Owns the ranked backlog and the next-task assignment for every repository in a fleet, and the numbered amendment log that makes the run reconstructable. Use when ranking work across repositories, answering a lane's request for its next item, ruling on a proposal, confirming a second lane's file split, or deciding whether an item is admissible at all. Normally runs as its own session; this file doubles as its brief. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/fleet-planner.md))
- **fleet-pr-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for one fleet PR at a time (review-fleet-prs skill). Answers with only the review JSON the launching prompt specifies. ([stSoftwareAU/VibeCoder](https://github.com/stSoftwareAU/VibeCoder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-pr-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **fleet-profiler**: Measures load, interaction and stability of a page in the parent session's pane and returns numbers with their spread and the machine load beside them. Use when a brief asks how slow, how janky, or what breaks over a long session. Holds the raw traces in its own context. ([makarasty/makarasty-claude](https://github.com/makarasty/makarasty-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-profiler.md))
- **fleet-quiet-bot**: Mention-only fleet chat adapter for addressed messages and bounded tasks. ([jmagly/aiwg](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg/blob/HEAD/agentic/code/addons/aiwg-fleet/agents/fleet-quiet-bot.md) · ★230)
- **fleet-recipe-deriver**: Derives a repository's Recipe — its Install step, Check step and Test entrypoint — by reading the repository, proves all three with recipe-prove.mjs, and reports. Dispatched by the run-team controller in phase 0, or by a standalone next-ticket or review-and-fix session, when the repository has no usable Recipe cache. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-recipe-deriver.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-release**: Owns the release-readiness board - for each product, what blocks its next release and the exact next action, with every claim proven. Use when asking what is shippable, what a promotion actually contains, what a human still has to decide, or when a blocker clears or appears. Turns a founder decision into one line with the proofs attached. Never merges, releases or deploys. Normally runs as its own session; this file doubles as its brief. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/fleet-release.md))
- **fleet-retrospective**: **Date:** 2026-07-21. **Scope:** an evidence-based review of the `fleet-orchestrator` / `fleet-worker` / `fleet-verifier` architecture (`.claude/agents/*.md`, `docs/agents/fleet-architecture.md`, `docs/agents/runbook.md`) as actually exercised across this project's build, not as designed on paper. **Why this document exists:** the user intends to carry this same architecture into later phases of a ([richardhcli/akasha](https://github.com/richardhcli/akasha/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/fleet-retrospective.md))
- **fleet-review-comments**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Review phase for the "comments" dimension. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-review-comments.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-review-correctness**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Review phase for the "correctness" dimension. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-review-correctness.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-review-runner**: A `/skill:run-team` review runner on omp — dispatched by the controller as review-pr-&lt;pr#&gt; (the PR's first runner; a later one takes the next free letter, review-pr-&lt;pr#&gt;-b, -c, …) with pr, branch, worktree, testCmd and scratch (the scratch root); runs runReviewOnOmp to completion in its own eval cell, writes &lt;scratch&gt;/pr&lt;pr&gt;/&lt;run&gt;/review.json in the review's own run root, and reports the digest, the path and the reviewed= token naming that run. - **fleet-review-silent-failure**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Review phase for the "silent-failure" dimension. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-review-silent-failure.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-review-simplify**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Review phase for the "simplify" dimension. Report-only — never edits a file. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-review-simplify.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-review-snapshot**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Snapshot phase to cut the immutable review snapshot and size the diff. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-review-snapshot.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-review-test-run**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Test run phase to run the review's test command once, from the snapshot's root, and report its counts. Never invoked directly. - **fleet-review-tests**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Review phase for the "tests" dimension. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-review-tests.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-review-types**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Review phase for the "types" dimension. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-review-types.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-review-verifier**: Dispatched by review-core.mjs's Verify phase to adversarially refute one finding. Never invoked directly. ([feigi/fleet-plugin](https://github.com/feigi/fleet-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-review-verifier.agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-reviewer**: Code reviewer with direct p2p dev communication. Two-pass review (code quality + acceptance criteria). Writes verdict to review.md. ([hubertciebiada/fleet-commander](https://github.com/hubertciebiada/fleet-commander/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-reviewer.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **fleet-reviewer**: Run before pushing a risky change (labelled safety-critical or security, or touching .github/, .claude/ other than a skill's references/ documents, .mcp.json, or the paths the app's second review lists), or when asked for a local review. Reviews the branch's change against origin/main with the same prompt CI's "Agent review" uses and answers with findings in the same shape. Read-only and advisory - CI's reviews decide. Issues APPROVE or BLOCK scoped to a head SHA, posted as a PR comment, naming the falsifier and the set. Normally runs as its own session; this file doubles as its brief. - **fleet-reviewer-security**: Fleet PR reviewer for security: auth, RBAC, PII, secrets, input validation. Diff-only. Used by the fleet-manager skill. ([klaidliadon/claude-plugins](https://github.com/klaidliadon/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/fleet/agents/fleet-reviewer-security.md))
- **fleet-reviewer-tests**: Fleet PR reviewer for test coverage and weakened assertions. Diff-only. Used by the fleet-manager skill. ([klaidliadon/claude-plugins](https://github.com/klaidliadon/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/fleet/agents/fleet-reviewer-tests.md))
- **fleet-round**: Run the machine half of ONE fleet round — read the router's step files yourself, run the sweep, the drive leg, the keep-alive leg, the own-parks leg, the open leg and the manager reap, persist the snapshot, and return the ≤ 1 KB verdict hand-back plus the payload path. Dispatched by router row 1 of /supervisor:fleet-drive. The manager keeps only the re-arm, the operator-facing rows and the render. - **fleet-rule-auditor**: Keeps the fleet's rules, role files, contracts and fleet definitions from turning into bureaucracy. Weighs every rule line by what it costs the seats against what it has prevented, and cuts, merges or shortens lines; it never adds one. Runs after every fleet run closes (mike starts it from the close step and closes the session once its turn is done), when the fleet text is over its budget, and on a draft definition before ready. ([temet-ai/mike](https://github.com/temet-ai/mike/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-rule-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **FLEET-RUNTIME**: What every transient fleet role (worker, merger, both reviewers, smoke-worker, epic-steward, design-answerer) does at startup and exit; role files point here. The architect (`role-opus-architect.md`) is interactive and skips the loop ceremonies (heartbeat, reservation check, per-iteration shutdown) but shares the cache read and the feedback file. ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/FLEET-RUNTIME.md) · ★10)
- **fleet-safety-reviewer**: Adversarial review of changes that reach the Nexus Qs in the field — through a flashed image or an OTA `apk upgrade` — against the repo's non-negotiables — no bricking write, every fix baked into its package with a pkgrel bump, OTA and A/B paths that cannot strand a unit, a unit keeps its identity, no personal access in public artifacts, stock parity for kernel/DTS work, device limits (2 cores, a 25 W amp). Use proactively before committing changes under pmos/, userspace/, kernel/, scripts/ or docker-build.sh. Read-only; returns ranked findings. - **fleet-scenario**: Walks a whole browser scenario in the parent session's pane and returns findings as bounded JSON, holding the screenshots and DOM reads in its own context. ([makarasty/makarasty-claude](https://github.com/makarasty/makarasty-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-scenario.md))
- **fleet-security**: Security audit agent. Checks for OWASP top 10 vulnerabilities, missing authentication, injection vectors, hardcoded secrets, and missing access control. - **fleet-sweep-reader**: Compute the fleet sweep's read half (Steps 0b–3 of /supervisor:fleet-loop) — read the four channels and the open-items ledger, diff against the previous snapshot, run the orphan reverse index, find collision and unmanaged-topic candidates, classify every session, persist the next snapshot through fleet-snapshot.py, write the digest render to its payload path, and return the ≤ 1 KB bucket-count hand-back. - **fleet-sync-agent**: Synchronization agent. Diffs repository spec state against Linear and Notion, reconciles differences. Repo always wins. Can be scheduled to run on a cron for continuous sync. ([shesha-io/shesha-plugins](https://github.com/shesha-io/shesha-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/shesha-developer-0-43/agents/fleet-transformer.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **fleet-verifier**: Independent verifier that re-runs a build-plan task's Verify command and cross-checks a fleet-worker's claim against real on-disk/git state before it can be marked DONE ([richardhcli/akasha](https://github.com/richardhcli/akasha/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-verifier.md))

- **fleet-watchdog**: You are the judgment layer over the ops-cycle's mechanical fleet checks. - **fleet-worker**: Use this agent for background fleet tasks dispatched by the coordinator. - **fleet-worker**: fleet 的执行会话——一个任务、一个 worktree、一个 PR：读任务记录 T&lt;n&gt;、实现、开 PR、门禁全绿后自己合并、收口 T 记录、向派它的会话回报（不认领、不评论、不关闭、不建普通 GitHub issue；唯一例外：会话仅可在 GuangZhouShanyouGame/24haowan-monorepo 通过仓库自带的 `node tools/cd/fragments-migrate.js --new &lt;file.md&gt;` 创建带 `release-fragment` 标签的 issue，随后按 `doc/release-ledger/fragment-schema.md` 更新这份 issue 的正文；认领、评论任务进度、关闭 issue、创建普通 issue 仍禁止，其他仓库不适用。第一句是人的原话，流程由 SessionStart hook 引入）。 ([verkyyi/claude-fleet](https://github.com/verkyyi/claude-fleet/blob/HEAD/conf/agent-defaults/claude/agents/fleet-worker.md) · ★2)
- **fleet-worker**: Executes ONE self-contained coding work order by delegating it to the local model fleet (your fleet) via the Crew Chief MCP tools, judges the result, and reports back. Use for single-file implementations against a clear spec — a converter, a parser, a small function, boilerplate, a batch classification task. Do NOT use for multi-file work, architecture, or anything needing repo-wide context — that's fleet-heavy or the calling agent's own job. ([chaio-dev/chaio-crewchief](https://github.com/chaio-dev/chaio-crewchief/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fleet-worker.md))
- **fleet-worker**: Task executor agent that owns exactly one build-plan task, decides whether to delegate to Cursor or edit directly, and always verifies results ([richardhcli/akasha](https://github.com/richardhcli/akasha/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-worker.md))
- **fleet-worker**: Executes one bounded implementation task inside a lane's worktree and reports exactly what it did, with commands and results. Use when a lane needs a scoped edit done without spending its own turn on it - dispatch in the background so the lane can keep heartbeating.
